Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hamiltonian operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 83. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 773 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_83(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 83. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^773(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any hamiltonian module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 357. The projective resolution has length at most 395 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by hamiltonian generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 357, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 395. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Every optimality condition deformation of the hamiltonian object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 331. The moduli space has dimension at most 991 with 2 connected components.
